<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Farmer Giles has two legs and each of his cows have four legs. In the picture below, there are six legs in total. 

Fill in the table below for the total number of legs if Farmer Giles is always present, but the number of cows increases.

|    Number of Cows    | $1$ | $2$ |  $3$ | $4$ | $10$ | $100$ |
|:--------------------:|:---:|:---:|:----:|:---:|:----:|:-----:|
| Total Number of Legs | $6$ |     | $14$ |     |      |       |

Hillary is trying to find a formula that links the Number of Cows, $C$, to the number of Legs, $L$. 

Fill in the gaps to help her. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Farmer Giles has two legs and each of his cows have four legs. In the picture below, there are six legs in total. 
<emptyline>

|    Number of Cows    | $1$ | $2$ |  $3$ | $4$ | $10$ | $100$ |
|:--------------------:|:---:|:---:|:----:|:---:|:----:|:-----:|
| Total Number of Legs | $6$ |     | $14$ |     |      |       |
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$\text{Total number of legs} = 4 \times \text{Number of cows} + 2$

$L = 4 \times C + 2$

|    Number of Cows    | $1$ |                 $2$                                |  $3$ |               $4$                                  |                $10$                                |                 $100$                             |
|:--------------------:|:---:|:---------------------------------:                 |:----:|:----------------------------------:                |:----------------------------------:                |:----------------------------------:               |
| Total Number of Legs | $6$ | $\green{4 \times 2 + 2 = 10}$     | $14$ | $\green{4 \times 4 + 2 = 18}$     | $\green{4 \times 10 + 2 = 42}$    | $\green{4 \times 100 + 2 = 402}$ |

Completed table:

|    Number of Cows    | $1$ |   $2$    |  $3$ |   $4$    |  $10$   | $100$ |
|:--------------------:|:---:|:--------:|:----:|:--------:|:-------:|:-----:|
| Total Number of Legs | $6$ | $10$     | $14$ | $18$     | $42$    | $402$ |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$L = 4 \times C + 2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i00.png](/assets/papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i00.png "papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i00.png")
![papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i01.png](/assets/papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i01.png "papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i01.png")
![papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i02.png](/assets/papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i02.png "papers/alleyns-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q20-i02.png")

This picture shows people sitting around tables made of smaller square tables. Complete the table below:


| Number of smaller tables  |  $1$   |   $2$   |  $3$   |   $4$   |  $10$  |  $100$   |
|:-------------------------:|:------:|:-------:|:------:|:-------:|:------:|:--------:|
|  Maximum number of people |        |   $6$   |        |   $10$  |        |          |

Adam is trying to find a formula that links the Table number, $T$, to the number of People, $P$. Fill in the gaps to help him:

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
%image%
%image%
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

| Number of smaller tables  |  $1$   |   $2$   |  $3$   |   $4$   |  $10$  |  $100$   |
|:-------------------------:|:------:|:-------:|:------:|:-------:|:------:|:--------:|
|  Maximum number of people |        |   $6$   |        |   $10$  |        |          |
<emptyline>
Adam is trying to find a formula that links the Table number, $T$, to the number of People, $P$. Fill in the gaps to help him:
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$\text{Maximum number of people} = 2 \times \text{Number of smaller tables} + 2$

$P = 2 \times T + 2$

| Number of smaller tables  |               $1$                             |   $2$   |             $3$                               |   $4$   |              $10$                               |                 $100$                             |
|:-------------------------:|:----------------------------:                 |:-------:|:----------------------------:                 |:-------:|:-------------------------------:                |:----------------------------------:               |
|  Maximum number of people | $\green{2 \times 1 + 2 = 4}$ |   $6$   | $\green{2 \times 3 + 2 = 8}$ |   $10$  | $\green{2 \times 10 + 2 = 22}$ | $\green{2 \times 100 + 2 = 202}$ |

Completed table:

| Number of smaller tables  | $1$ |   $2$   | $3$ |   $4$   | $10$ | $100$ |
|:-------------------------:|:--: |:-------:|:---:|:-------:|:--:  |:----: |
|  Maximum number of people | $4$ |   $6$   | $8$ |   $10$  | $22$ | $202$ |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$P = 2 \times T + 2$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

|    $\ 2$  |  $\ldots$  |   $\ldots$  |
|---------|:----------:|:-----------:|

|    $2$    |  $\ldots$  |      $4$    |

**Question**

Sam is completing a table of values for the formula $2(n + 1)$.

He has completed the first row of the table.

|   $n$  | $2(n+1)$ |
|:------:|:--------:|

|  $19$  |          |
|        |   $80$   |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

This diagram shows the reults of a class survey concerning pets.

|                               | Boys  | Girls         | Total         |
|:---------------------:        |:----: |:-----:        |:-----:        |
| Have at least $1$ pet         | $11$  |               |               |
|      Have no pets             |       |  $8$          |               |
|         Total                 | $16$  |               |  $30$         |

How many children have at least one pet?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">This diagram shows the reults of a class survey concerning pets.
<emptyline>
|                               | Boys  | Girls         | Total         |
|:---------------------:        |:----: |:-----:        |:-----:        |
| Have at least $1$ pet         | $11$  |               |               |
|      Have no pets             |       |  $8$          |               |
|         Total                 | $16$  |               |  $30$         |
<emptyline>
How many children have at least one pet?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's complete the table.

|                               |      Boys                                                                            |        Girls                                                                         |      Total                                                                           |
|:-----------------------------:|:------------------------------------:                                                |:------------------------------------:                                                |:------------------------------------:                                                |
| Have at least $1$ pet         |      $11$                                                                            |    $\green{14 - 8 = 6} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(3)}}$ |  $\green{11 + 6 = 17}  \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(4)}}$ |
|      Have no pets             | $\green{16 - 11 = 5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(2)}}$   |         $8$                                                                          | $\green{30 - 17 = 13}  \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(5)}}$ |
|         Total                 |      $16$                                                                            | $\green{30 - 16 = 14} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(1)}}$  |      $30$                                                                            |

Completed table:

|                               |      Boys  |  Girls |  Total  |
|:-----------------------------:|:---------: |:-----: |:-------:|
| Have at least $1$ pet         | $11$       | $6$    |  $17$   |
|      Have no pets             | $5$        | $8$    |  $13$   |
|         Total                 | $16$       | $14$   |  $30$   |

Children with atleast $1$ pet $= 17$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A group of children are playing a game where they can score either $2, 3$ or $5$ points on each turn. 
The children have had $8$ turns each and recorded their scores in a table, but some of the entries have been deleted. 
Fill in the missing numbers to give a complete set of scores.

| **Name**    | **$2$ points** | **$3$ points** | **$5$ points** | **Total Score** |
|:-----------:|:--------------:|:--------------:|:--------------:|:---------------:|
| Carl        |       $1$      |       $6$      |       $1$      |       $25$      |
| Alex        |       $4$      |       $2$      |                |                 |
| Yusuf       |                |       $3$      |                |       $25$      |
| Jing        |                |                |                |       $35$      |
| Charlotte   |                |                |                |       $18$      |

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The vehicles listed below were seen driving past the front of Reigate Grammar School between $4.00 \ \text{pm}$ and $4.15 \ \text{pm}$ last night.

| $2$ coaches each with $52$ passengers and a  driver   | $2\times53=$   | $106$     |
|------------------------------------------------------ |:-----------:  |:-----:    |
| $5$ lorries each with a driver only                   |               |           |
| $3$ minibuses each with $15$ passengers and a driver  |               |           |
| $6$ cars with only a driver                           |               |           |
| $4$ cars with a driver and $1$ passenger each         |               |           |
| $5$ cars with a driver and $2$ passengers each        |               |           |

Fill in the table and use your table to find;

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

| $2$ coaches each with $52$ passengers and a  driver   | $2 \times 53$                          | $106$                              |
|------------------------------------------------------ |:-----------:                           |:-----:                             |
| $5$ lorries each with a driver only                   | $\green{5 \times 1}$  |   $\green{5}$     |
| $3$ minibuses each with $15$ passengers and a driver  | $\green{3 \times 16}$ |   $\green{48}$    |
| $6$ cars with only a driver                           | $\green{6 \times 1}$  |    $\green{6}$    |
| $4$ cars with a driver and $1$ passenger each         | $\green{4 \times 2}$  |   $\green{8}$     |
| $5$ cars with a driver and $2$ passengers each        | $\green{5 \times 3}$  |   $\green{15}$    |

Completed table:

| $2$ coaches each with $52$ passengers and a  driver   | $2 \times 53$ | $106$     |
|------------------------------------------------------ |:-----------:  |:-----:    |
| $5$ lorries each with a driver only                   | $5 \times 1$  |   $5$     |
| $3$ minibuses each with $15$ passengers and a driver  | $3 \times 16$ |   $48$    |
| $6$ cars with only a driver                           | $6 \times 1$  |    $6$    |
| $4$ cars with a driver and $1$ passenger each         | $4 \times 2$  |   $8$     |
| $5$ cars with a driver and $2$ passengers each        | $5 \times 3$  |   $15$    |

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of vehicles passed}        &= 2 + 5 + 3 + 6 + 4 + 5 \\\\
                                        &= 25
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a dancing competition, the four judges gave marks ranging from $–5$ to $5$, (whole numbers). There were $4$ competitors.

Andrew got top marks from every judge. 

Brian's score was $4$ less than Andrew. 

Carla and Donna both had the same final score of $7$.

Complete the table:

|               | Judge $1$     | Judge $2$     | Judge $3$     | Judge $4$     | Total         |
|--------       |:---------:    |:---------:    |:---------:    |:---------:    |:-----:        |
| Andrew        |               |               |               |               |               |
| Brian         |    $5$        |    $4$        |    $3$        |               |               |
| Carla         |    $4$        |    $2$        |               |    $0$        |               |
| Donna         |    $0$        |    $-3$       |    $5$        |               |               |  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a dancing competition, the four judges gave marks ranging from $–5$ to $5$, (whole numbers). There were $4$ competitors.
<emptyline>
Andrew got top marks from every judge. 
<emptyline>
Brian's score was $4$ less than Andrew. 
<emptyline>
Carla and Donna both had the same final score of $7$.
<emptyline>
Complete the table:
<emptyline>
|               | Judge $1$     | Judge $2$     | Judge $3$     | Judge $4$     | Total         |
|--------       |:---------:    |:---------:    |:---------:    |:---------:    |:-----:        |
| Andrew        |               |               |               |               |               |
| Brian         |    $5$        |    $4$        |    $3$        |               |               |
| Carla         |    $4$        |    $2$        |               |    $0$        |               |
| Donna         |    $0$        |    $-3$       |    $5$        |               |               |  
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's complete the table.

|               | Judge $1$                                                                     | Judge $2$                                                                  | Judge $3$                                                                              | Judge $4$                                                                                                 | Total                                                                                       |
|--------       |:---------:                                                                    |:---------:                                                                 |:---------:                                                                             |:---------:                                                                                                |:-----:                                                                                      |
| Andrew        |    $\green{5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(1)}}$   |  $\green{5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(2)}}$  |   $\green{5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(3)}}$             |  $\green{5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(4)}}$                                 |  $\green{5 + 5 + 5 + 5 = 20} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(5)}}$  |
| Brian         |              $5$                                                              |             $4$                                                            |              $3$                                                                       |  $\green{16 - 5 - 4 - 3 = 4} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(7)}}$                |   $\green{20 - 4 = 16} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(6)}}$        |
| Carla         |              $4$                                                              |             $2$                                                            |  $\green{7 - 4 - 2 = 1} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(9)}}$  |                  $0$                                                                                      |  $\green{7} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(8)}}$                   |
| Donna         |              $0$                                                              |             $-3$                                                           |              $5$                                                                       |  $\green{7 - 0 - (-3) - 5 = 7 + 3 - 5 = 5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(11)}}$ |  $\green{7} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(10)}}$                  |

| Brian         |    $5$        |    $4$        |    $3$        |    $4$        |    $16$       |
| Carla         |    $4$        |    $2$        |    $1$        |    $0$        |     $7$       |
| Donna         |    $0$        |    $-3$       |    $5$        |    $5$        |     $7$       |

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many goals were scored altogether?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total goals scored}       &= 4 \times 10 + 2 \times 10 + 1.5 \times 10 + 2.5 \times 10 \\\\
                                &= 40 + 20 + 15 + 25 \\\\
                                &= 90
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

Raiders?

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}       &= 4 \times 10 - 2.5 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 40 - 25 \\\\
                        &= 15
\end{aligned}
$

The Shooters scored $15$ goals more than the Raiders.

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}       &= 4 \times 10 - 2.5 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 40 - 25 \\\\
                        &= 15
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The Shooters scored $15$ goals more than the Raiders.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$6$ dogs were weighed, and then one year later they were weighed again.

A weight gain is shown as a positive number and a loss is shown as a negative number. 

For example $-3$ means that the dog lost $3 \ \text{kilograms}$ in weight.

| dog name | weight change, in kg |
|:--------:|:--------------------:|
|    Rex   |         $+1$         |
|    Sam   |         $-2$         |
|   Troy   |         $+2$         |
|  Walker  |          $0$         |
|   Yogi   |         $-3$         |
|    Zig   |         $-1$         |

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which class has a higher proportion of boys?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which class has a higher proportion of boys?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Proportion of boys in 6A}         &= \dfrac {14} {30} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {14 \times 3} {30 \times 3} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {42} {90} \\\\
\text{Proportion of boys in 6B}         &= \dfrac {12} {18} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {12 \times 5} {18 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {60} {90}
\end{aligned}
$

$6B$ has higher proportion of boys.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6B$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What fraction of the children in $6B$ are girls? 

Give your answer in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What fraction of the children in $6B$ are girls? 
<emptyline>
Give your answer in its simplest form.
</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

%empty%
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Proportion of girls in 6B}        &= \dfrac {6} {18} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {6} {6 \times 3} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel 6} {\cancel 6 \times 3} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of boys who left the school $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {48 - a} {2}             &= 26 - a \\\\
48 - a                          &= 2 \times (26 - a) \\\\
48 - a                          &= 2 \times 26 - 2a \\\\
48 - a                          &= 52 - 2a \\\\
2a - a                          &= 52 - 48 \\\\
a                               &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

Number of boys who left the school $= 4$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

There are $80$ members of the school orchestra.

$35$ of the members are boys.

$\dfrac{2}{3}$ of the girls in the orchestra play a stringed instrument.

In total, $38$ of the members do not play a stringed instrument.

Use this information to complete the table below.

|                                     | boy | girl | total |
|:-----------------------------------:|:---:|:----:|:-----:|
|     plays a stringed instrument     |     |      |       |
| does not play a stringed instrument |     |      |       |
|                total                |     |      |  $80$ |

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There are $80$ members of the school orchestra.
<emptyline>
$35$ of the members are boys.
<emptyline>
$\dfrac{2}{3}$ of the girls in the orchestra play a stringed instrument.
<emptyline>
In total, $38$ of the members do not play a stringed instrument.
<emptyline>
Use this information to complete the table below.
<emptyline>
|                                     | boy | girl | total |
|:-----------------------------------:|:---:|:----:|:-----:|
|     plays a stringed instrument     |     |      |       |
| does not play a stringed instrument |     |      |       |
|                total                |     |      |  $80$ |
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

|                                     |                boy                                                                  |                    girl                                                                              |                   total                                                             |
|:-----------------------------------:|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------:|:----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------:|:-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------:|
|     plays a stringed instrument     | $\green{42 - 30 = 12} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(7)}}$ | $\green{\dfrac {2} {3} \times 45 = 30} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(3)}}$ | $\green{80 - 38 = 42} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(6)}}$ |
| does not play a stringed instrument | $\green{35 - 12 = 23} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(8)}}$ |          $\green{45 - 30 = 15} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(4)}}$         |       $\green{38} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(5)}}$     |
|                total                |     $\green{35} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(1)}}$       |          $\green{80 - 35 = 45} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(2)}}$         |                 $80$                                                                |

Completed table:

|                                     | boy | girl | total |
|:-----------------------------------:|:---:|:----:|:-----:|
|     plays a stringed instrument     |$12$ | $30$ |  $42$ |
| does not play a stringed instrument |$23$ | $15$ |  $38$ |
|                total                |$35$ | $45$ |  $80$ |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

**Question**

The number machine below adds $3$ to the input number and then multiplies by $2$.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q14-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q14-s01-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q14-s01-i00.png")

Complete the input and output table for this machine.

| **input** | **output** |
|:---------:|:----------:|
|    $1$    |     $8$    |
|    $0$    |  $\ldots$  |
|  $\ldots$ |    $14$    |

</div>

</code></pre>

|    $1$    |     $1 \times 2 + 3 = 2 + 3 = 5$    |
|    $2$    |     $2 \times 2 + 3 = 4 + 3 = 7$    |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On the Gagalapos Islands, there lives an amazing animal called the Joak.

When it is born, the Joak has one head with a bristle, and one body segment with two pairs of bristles, as shown:

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q30-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q30-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q30-i00.png")

At the start of each year, Joak grows one more identical body segment so that at the start of the second year it looks like this: 

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q30-i01.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q30-i01.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-2/s01-q30-i01.png")

</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many bristles will Joak have during its $10 ^\text{th}$ year? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many bristles will Joak have during its $10 ^\text{th}$ year? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bristles}       &= 10 \times 4 + 1 \\\\
                                &= 40 + 1 \\\\
                                &= 41
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$41$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of bristles}       &= 10 \times 4 + 1 \\\\
                                &= 40 + 1 \\\\
                                &= 41
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In which year will Joak have $65$ bristles?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In which year will Joak have $65$ bristles?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the year $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 4 + 1          &= 65 \\\\
4a                      &= 65 - 1 \\\\
4a                      &= 64 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {64} {4} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 16} {4} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 16} {\cancel 4} \\\\
a                       &= 16
\end{aligned}
$

The year in which Joak will have $65$ bristles $= 16$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Amy has the two-stage number machine shown below.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q15-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q15-s01-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q15-s01-i00.png")

Complete the table of input and output numbers for Amy's machine.

| **input** | **output** |
|:---------:|:----------:|
|    $2$    |     $0$    |
|    $5$    |            |
|           |    $-2$    |
|   $-2$    |            |

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Hannah has a different number machine which has produced the following table of input and output numbers.

| **input** | **output** |
|:---------:|:----------:|
|  $-1$     |    $-2$    |
|   $0$     |    $-2$    |
|   $1$     |    $4$     |
|   $3$     |    $10$    |

Unfortunately the labels have fallen off Hannah's machine.

Write suitable labels on the diagram below.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q15-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q15-s02-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-2/s01-q15-s02-i00.png")

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Hannah has a different number machine which has produced the following table of input and output numbers.
<emptyline>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$\text{Input} \times 3 + 1 = \text{Output}$

Let's verify.

| **input** |             **output**              |
|:---------:|:-----------------------------------:|
|  $-1$     |    $-1 \times 3 + 1 = -3 + 1 = -2$  |
|   $0$     |    $0 \times 3 + 1 = 0 + 1 = 1$     |
|   $1$     |    $1 \times 3 + 1 = 3 + 1 = 4$     |
|   $3$     |    $3 \times 3 + 1 = 9 + 1 = 10$    |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{Input} \times 3 + 1 = \text{Output}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a quiz with $20$ questions, contestants are awarded $3$ points for a correct answer, but lose $2$ points for each wrong answer.

Complete the table for the contestants below.

The first one has already been done for you.

|   name  | number of correct answers | number of wrong answers | total points |
|:-------:|:-------------------------:|:-----------------------:|:------------:|
| Rebecca |            $7$            |           $13$          |     $-5$     |
|  Safiya |            $14$           |                         |              |
|  Tosin  |                           |           $16$          |              |
|  Ursula |                           |                         |      $0$     |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a quiz with $20$ questions, contestants are awarded $3$ points for a correct answer, but lose $2$ points for each wrong answer.
<emptyline>
Complete the table for the contestants below.
<emptyline>
The first one has already been done for you.
<emptyline>
|   name  | number of correct answers | number of wrong answers | total points |
|:-------:|:-------------------------:|:-----------------------:|:------------:|
| Rebecca |            $7$            |           $13$          |     $-5$     |
|  Safiya |            $14$           |                         |              |
|  Tosin  |                           |           $16$          |              |
|  Ursula |                           |                         |      $0$     |
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of correct answers by Ursula $= a$

Number of wrong answers by Ursula $= 20 - a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 3 - (20 - a) \times 2          &= 0 \\\\
3a - 20 \times 2 + 2a                   &= 0 \\\\
5a - 40                                 &= 0 \\\\
5a                                      &= 40 \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {40} {5} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {5 \times 8} {5} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 8} {\cancel 5} \\\\
a                                       &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

Let's complete the table.

|   name  | number of correct answers                                                          | number of wrong answers                                                             | total points                                                                                                    |
|:-------:|:----------------------------------:                                                |:-----------------------------------:                                                |:---------------------------------------------------------------:                                                |
| Rebecca |            $7$                                                                     |           $13$                                                                      |          $-5$                                                                                                   |
|  Safiya |            $14$                                                                    | $\green{20 - 14 = 6} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(2)}}$  | $\green{14 \times 3 - 6 \times 2 = 42 - 12 = 30} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(3)}}$  |
|  Tosin  | $\green{20 - 16 = 4} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(1)}}$ |           $16$                                                                      | $\green{4 \times 3 - 16 \times 2 = 12 - 32 = -20} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(4)}}$ |
|  Ursula |      $\green{8} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(5)}}$      |  $\green{20 - 8 = 12} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(6)}}$ |          $0$                                                                                                    |

Completed table:

|   name  | number of correct answers | number of wrong answers | total points |
|:-------:|:-------------------------:|:-----------------------:|:------------:|
| Rebecca |            $7$            |           $13$          |     $-5$     |
|  Safiya |            $14$           |           $6$           |     $30$     |
|  Tosin  |           $4$             |           $16$          |     $-20$    |
|  Ursula |           $8$             |           $12$          |      $0$     |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

James missed the $4.20 \ \text{p.m.}$ train by three minutes.

|             |       Train A      |       Train B      |
|:-----------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|
|    London   | $4.20 \ \text{p.m.}$ | $5.02 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
| Staplehurst | $5.16 \ \text{p.m.}$ | $5.59 \ \text{p.m.}$ |

How long must he wait to catch the $5.02 \ \text{p.m.}$ train?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">James missed the $4.20 \ \text{p.m.}$ train by three minutes.
<emptyline>
|             |       Train A      |       Train B      |
|:-----------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|
|    London   | $4.20 \ \text{p.m.}$ | $5.02 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
| Staplehurst | $5.16 \ \text{p.m.}$ | $5.59 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
<emptyline>
How long must he wait to catch the $5.02 \ \text{p.m.}$ train?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time when James reached the station}      &= 4{:}20 + 3 \ \text{mins} \\\\
                                                &= 4{:}23 \\\\
\text{Waiting time}                             &= 5{:}02 - 4{:}23 \\\\
                                                &= 39 \ \text{mins}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$39 \ \text{mins}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time when James reached the station}      &= 4{:}20 + 3 \ \text{mins} \\\\
                                                &= 4{:}23 \\\\
\text{Waiting time}                             &= 5{:}02 - 4{:}23 \\\\
                                                &= 39 \ \text{mins}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Daniel and Bella are playing a game.

When Daniel calls out a number, Bella multiplies it by $3$ and then subtracts $5$ and writes down the result.

For example, when Daniel calls out $2$, Bella writes down $1$.

They record the numbers in a table.

Complete the table below.

| Daniel calls out | Bella writes down |
|:----------------:|:-----------------:|
|        $5$       |                   |
|                  |        $19$       |
|                  |        $-2$       |

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Daniel and Bella are playing a game.
<emptyline>
When Daniel calls out a number, Bella multiplies it by $3$ and then subtracts $5$ and writes down the result.
<emptyline>
For example, when Daniel calls out $2$, Bella writes down $1$.
<emptyline>
They record the numbers in a table.
<emptyline>
Complete the table below.
<emptyline>
| Daniel calls out | Bella writes down |
|:----------------:|:-----------------:|
|        $5$       |                   |
|                  |        $19$       |
|                  |        $-2$       |
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

| Daniel calls out                                                                                                 | Bella writes down                                                                                   |
|:----------------------------------------------------------------:                                                |:---------------------------------------------------:                                                |
|                                $5$                                                                               | $\green{5 \times 3 - 5 = 15 - 5 = 10} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(1)}}$ |
| $\green{\dfrac {19 + 5} {3} = \dfrac {24} {3} = 8} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(2)}}$ |                          $19$                                                                       |
| $\green{\dfrac {-2 + 5} {3} = \dfrac {3} {3} = 1} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(3)}}$  |                          $-2$                                                                       |

Completed table:

| Daniel calls out | Bella writes down |
|:----------------:|:-----------------:|
|        $5$       |        $10$       |
|        $8$       |        $19$       |
|        $1$       |        $-2$       |
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a skate-boarding competition competitors do a series of tricks which judges score as follows:-

Super scores **$5$ points**

Good scores **$3$ points**

Poor scores **$2$ points**

After a few tricks the scores are put up on a board with the competitors in order of their total score. Unfortunately it started to rain and some of the figures were then washed out.

| **Name**| **No of tricks** | **Super** | **Good** | **Poor** | **Total Points** |
|:-------:|:------------:|:-----:|:----:|:----:|:------------:|
|  Derek  |       $8$      |         |   $1$  |   $2$  |                |
|   Ali   |       $9$      |         |        |        |                |
| Charlie |       $9$      |   $2$   |        |        |      $30$      |
|  Brian  |                |   $4$   |        |        |      $23$      |

Fill in all the missing numbers on the board so that the judges have a complete set of scores.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

Super scores **$5$ points**
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
| **Name**| **No of tricks** | **Super** | **Good** | **Poor** | **Total Points** |
|:-------:|:------------:|:-----:|:----:|:----:|:------------:|
|  Derek  |       $8$      |         |   $1$  |   $2$  |                |
|   Ali   |       $9$      |         |        |        |                |
| Charlie |       $9$      |   $2$   |        |        |      $30$      |
|  Brian  |                |   $4$   |        |        |      $23$      |
<emptyline>
Fill in all the missing numbers on the board so that the judges have a complete set of scores.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Each day a shoal of small fish increases in number by $50\%$ but each night a shark eats $300$ of them. 

So if there are $1000$ fish on Monday morning, then there will be $1500$ by Monday evening but only $1200$ left by Tuesday morning!

**Complete the following tables showing the number of fish each morning.** 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If the number of fish each morning is the same on each of the four days, how many fish were there on Monday morning?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the number of fish each morning is the same on each of the four days, how many fish were there on Monday morning?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of fish on monday $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + \dfrac {50} {100} \times a - 300            &= a \\\\
a - a + \dfrac {50} {100} \times a - 300        &= 0 \\\\
\dfrac {50a} {100} - 300                        &= 0 \\\\
\dfrac {50a} {100}                              &= 300 \\\\
a                                               &= 300 \times \dfrac {100} {50} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {300 \times 100} {50} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {300 \times 50 \times 2} {50} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {300 \times \cancel {50} \times 2} {\cancel {50}} \\\\
a                                               &= 300 \times 2 \\\\
a                                               &= 600
\end{aligned}
$

Number of fish on monday $= 600$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$600$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the MGS running competition, runners are placed in five heats and their time and position in their heat is used to work out when they can start in the final **Rusholme Rally** race.

The results in the heats were as follows, the times are all in minutes and seconds

|          |     Heat $1$       |     Heat $2$       |     Heat $3$       |     Heat $4$       |     Heat $5$       |
|:--------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|
| Position |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |
|   $1^\text{st}$  | $\text{A} \ 1\text{m} \ 45 \text{s}$ | $\text{D} \ 1\text{m} \ 30 \text{s}$ | $\text{G} \ 2\text{m} \ 05 \text{s}$ | $\text{J} \ 1\text{m} \ 40 \text{s}$ | $\text{M} \ 1\text{m} \ 35 \text{s}$ |
|   $2^\text{nd}$   | $\text{B} \ 2\text{m} \ 03 \text{s}$ | $\text{E} \ 1\text{m} \ 58 \text{s}$ | $\text{H} \ 2\text{m} \ 20 \text{s}$ | $\text{K} \ 1\text{m} \ 50 \text{s}$ | $\text{N} \ 1\text{m} \ 55 \text{s}$ |
|   $3^\text{rd}$  | $\text{C} \ 2\text{m} \ 30 \text{s}$ | $\text{F} \ 2\text{m} \ 25 \text{s}$ | $\text{I} \ 2\text{m} \ 50 \text{s}$ | $\text{L} \ 1\text{m} \ 59 \text{s}$ | $\text{O} \ 2\text{m} \ 40 \text{s}$ |

In the final **Rusholme Rally** race, the winner of each heat is given a $20 \ \text{second}$ handicap, 
the second place runner is given a $10 \ \text{second}$ handicap and any runner with a time faster than two minutes is given an **extra** $5 \ \text{second}$ 
handicap. This means that in the final **Rusholme Rally** race, runners with no handicap set off when the start is signalled. 
Any runner with a $5 \ \text{second}$ handicap sets off $5 \ \text{seconds}$ after the start and similarly for the other time handicaps.

</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

| Position |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |     Runner Time    |
|   $1^\text{st}$  | $\text{A} \ 1\text{m} \ 45 \text{s}$ | $\text{D} \ 1\text{m} \ 30 \text{s}$ | $\text{G} \ 2\text{m} \ 05 \text{s}$ | $\text{J} \ 1\text{m} \ 40 \text{s}$ | $\text{M} \ 1\text{m} \ 35 \text{s}$ |
|   $2^\text{nd}$   | $\text{B} \ 2\text{m} \ 03 \text{s}$ | $\text{E} \ 1\text{m} \ 58 \text{s}$ | $\text{H} \ 2\text{m} \ 20 \text{s}$ | $\text{K} \ 1\text{m} \ 50 \text{s}$ | $\text{N} \ 1\text{m} \ 55 \text{s}$ |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On  $\text{January} \ 1 ^ \text{st} \ 2013$ a new spymaster recruits $4$ new spies. 
On $\text{January} \ 1 ^ \text{st}$ every following year he recruits twice as many new spies as he did the previous year.

Exactly two years after being recruited each spy recruits two new spies and each year after that recruits twice as many 
as the year before, so the four spies recruited by the spymaster 
in $2013$ would recruit a total of eight new spies in $2015$ as shown in the second column of the table. 
These eight spies would then recruit $16$ new spies in $2016$. Also, the eight spies recruited by the spymaster in $2014$ would recruit 
$16$ new spies in $2016$ which is why the entry in the second column for $2016$ is a **__total__** of $32$.

Complete the table of spies recruited by the spymaster and his spies up to $2018$, putting an answer on the dotted line in each of the spaces below.

|        | New spies recruited that year by spymaster | New spies recruited that year by other spies | TOTAL number of new spies recruited that year | TOTAL number of spies recruited since $2013$ |
|:------:|:------------------------------------------:|:--------------------------------------------:|:---------------------------------------------:|:------------------------------------------:|
| $2013$ |                     $4$                    |                      $0$                     |                      $4$                      |                     $4$                    |
| $2014$ |                     $8$                    |                      $0$                     |                      $8$                      |                    $12$                    |
| $2015$ |                     $16$                   |                      $8$                     |                    $\ldots$                   |                  $\ldots$                  |
| $2016$ |                   $\ldots$                 |                     $32$                     |                    $\ldots$                   |                    $100$                   |
| $2017$ |                     $64$                   |                   $\ldots$                   |                    $\ldots$                   |                    $260$                   |
| $2018$ |                   $\ldots$                 |                     $256$                    |                    $\ldots$                   |                    $644$                   |

</div>

<div class="papername">
<p>the-manchester-grammar-school--11-plus--maths--2017--arithmetic-b:1:3</p>

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The usual Olympic medal table, like the one used in Rio in $2016$, simply ranks countries in order of the number of Gold medals won.

However, the table below has been created by giving a point score to each type of medal won. The number of points awarded for each of the medals in this table is as follows:- $3$ points for every Gold medal won, $2$ points for each Silver medal won and $1$ point for a Bronze medal won.

**Complete the table below by filling in the missing numbers of medals** using the point scores given to the Gold, Silver and Bronze medals. 

**You need to know that the country of Esthopia won equal numbers of Gold, Silver and Bronze medals in order to complete the final line.**

{/*
Country     ,Gold $(3 \text{pts})$      ,Silver $(2 \text{pts})$    ,Bronze $(1 \text{pt})$     ,Points (Pts)
Abalasia    ,$17$                       ,$10$                       ,$8$                        ,$79$
Brussland   ,$13$                       ,$8$                        ,$16$                       ,
Ceeden      ,$14$                       ,$9$                        ,                           ,$65$
Dorway      ,                           ,$8$                        ,$7$                        ,$62$
Esthopia    ,                           ,                           ,                           ,$60$
*/}

|  Country  | Gold ($3$ pts) | Silver ($2$ pts) | Bronze ($1$ pts) | Points (Pts) |
|:---------:|:---------------------:|:-----------------------:|:----------------------:|:------------:|
|  Abalasia |          $17$         |           $10$          |           $8$          |     $79$     |
| Brussland |          $13$         |           $8$           |          $16$          |              |
|   Ceeden  |          $14$         |           $9$           |                        |     $65$     |
|   Dorway  |                       |           $8$           |           $7$          |     $62$     |
|  Esthopia |                       |                         |                        |     $60$     |

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The usual Olympic medal table, like the one used in Rio in $2016$, simply ranks countries in order of the number of Gold medals won.
<emptyline>
However, the table below has been created by giving a point score to each type of medal won. The number of points awarded for each of the medals in this table is as follows:- $3$ points for every Gold medal won, $2$ points for each Silver medal won and $1$ point for a Bronze medal won.
<emptyline>
**Complete the table below by filling in the missing numbers of medals** using the point scores given to the Gold, Silver and Bronze medals. 
<emptyline>
**You need to know that the country of Esthopia won equal numbers of Gold, Silver and Bronze medals in order to complete the final line.**
<emptyline>
{/*
Country     ,Gold $(3 \text{pts})$      ,Silver $(2 \text{pts})$    ,Bronze $(1 \text{pt})$     ,Points (Pts)
Abalasia    ,$17$                       ,$10$                       ,$8$                        ,$79$
Brussland   ,$13$                       ,$8$                        ,$16$                       ,
Ceeden      ,$14$                       ,$9$                        ,                           ,$65$
Dorway      ,                           ,$8$                        ,$7$                        ,$62$
Esthopia    ,                           ,                           ,                           ,$60$
*/}
<emptyline>
|  Country  | Gold ($3$ pts) | Silver ($2$ pts) | Bronze ($1$ pts) | Points (Pts) |
|:---------:|:---------------------:|:-----------------------:|:----------------------:|:------------:|
|  Abalasia |          $17$         |           $10$          |           $8$          |     $79$     |
| Brussland |          $13$         |           $8$           |          $16$          |              |
|   Ceeden  |          $14$         |           $9$           |                        |     $65$     |
|   Dorway  |                       |           $8$           |           $7$          |     $62$     |
|  Esthopia |                       |                         |                        |     $60$     |
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of gold points earned by Esthopia $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3 \times a + 2 \times a + a             &= 60 \\\\
3a + 2a + a                             &= 60 \\\\
6a                                      &= 60 \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {60} {6} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {6 \times 10} {6} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 10} {\cancel 6} \\\\
a                                       &= 10
\end{aligned}
$

Let's complete the table.

|  Country  | Gold $(3 \text{pts})$                                                                                                                                     | Silver $(2 \text{pts})$                                                    | Bronze $(1 \text{pt})$                                                                                                  | Points (Pts)                                                                                                          |
|:---------:|:---------------------:                                                                                                                                    |:-----------------------:                                                   |:----------------------:                                                                                                 |:------------:                                                                                                         |
|  Abalasia |          $17$                                                                                                                                             |           $10$                                                             |           $8$                                                                                                           |     $79$                                                                                                              |
| Brussland |          $13$                                                                                                                                             |           $8$                                                              |          $16$                                                                                                           | $\green{13 \times 3 + 8 \times 2 + 16 = 71} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(1)}}$             |
|   Ceeden  |          $14$                                                                                                                                             |           $9$                                                              | $\green{65 - 14 \times 3 - 9 \times 2 = 65 - 42 - 18 = 5} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(2)}}$ |     $65$                                                                                                              |
|   Dorway  | $\green{\dfrac {62 - 8 \times 2 - 7} {3} = \dfrac {62 - 16 - 7} {3} = \dfrac {39} {3} = 13} \ \maroonC{\footnotesize{(3)}}$ |           $8$                                                              |           $7$                                                                                                           |     $62$                                                                                                              |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The following table shows the first three rows of a table which has five columns. The **__values__** of all the entries on a particular row are the same but they are expressed in different forms.


{/*
        ,                           ,               ,           ,           ,
Row 1   ,$1^{3}$                    ,$1$            ,$1$        ,$1^{2}$    ,$1^{2}$
Row 2   ,$1^{3} + 2^{3}$            ,$1 + 8$        ,$9$        ,$3^{2}$    ,$(1 + 2)^{2}$
Row 3   ,$1^{3} + 2^{3} + 3^{3}$    ,$1 + 8 + 27$   ,$36$       ,$6^{2}$    ,$(1 + 2 + 3)^{2}$
Row 4   ,$a)$                       ,               ,$b)$       ,           ,
Row 5   ,                           ,$c)$           ,           ,           ,$d)$
Row 6   ,                           ,               ,$e)$       ,           ,
*/}

| Row $1$ |         $1^{3}$         |      $1$     |  $1$ | $1^{2}$ |      $1^{2}$      |
|:-------:|:-----------------------:|:------------:|:----:|:-------:|:-----------------:|
| Row $2$ |     $1^{3} + 2^{3}$ |    $1 + 8$  |  $9$ | $3^{2}$ |   $(1 + 2)^{2}$   |
| Row $3$ | $1^{3} + 2^{3} + 3^{3}$  | $1 + 8 + 27$ | $36$ | $6^{2}$ | $(1 + 2 + 3)^{2}$ |
| Row $4$ | $\footnotesize{\maroonC{a)}}$  | | $\footnotesize{\maroonC{b)}}$ |  | |
| Row $5$ |  |$\footnotesize{\maroonC{c)}}$ |  |  |        $\footnotesize{\maroonC{d)}}$ |
| Row $6$ |  |   | $\footnotesize{\maroonC{e)}}$ |  |   |

**__Without necessarily completing__** the entire table, write down the **__entry__** that would be written in the space in the table denoted by each of the letters.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The following table shows the first three rows of a table which has five columns. The **__values__** of all the entries on a particular row are the same but they are expressed in different forms.
<emptyline>

{/*

Row 3   ,$1^{3} + 2^{3} + 3^{3}$    ,$1 + 8 + 27$   ,$36$       ,$6^{2}$    ,$(1 + 2 + 3)^{2}$
Row 4   ,$a)$                       ,               ,$b)$       ,           ,
Row 5   ,                           ,$c)$           ,           ,           ,$d)$

*/}
<emptyline>
| Row $1$ |         $1^{3}$         |      $1$     |  $1$ | $1^{2}$ |      $1^{2}$      |
|:-------:|:-----------------------:|:------------:|:----:|:-------:|:-----------------:|
| Row $2$ |     $1^{3} + 2^{3}$ |    $1 + 8$  |  $9$ | $3^{2}$ |   $(1 + 2)^{2}$   |
| Row $3$ | $1^{3} + 2^{3} + 3^{3}$  | $1 + 8 + 27$ | $36$ | $6^{2}$ | $(1 + 2 + 3)^{2}$ |
| Row $4$ | $\footnotesize{\rtq_abbr_working_color_info{a)}}$  | | $\footnotesize{\rtq_abbr_working_color_info{b)}}$ |  | |

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 ^ 3 + 2 ^ 3 + 3 ^ 3 + 4 ^ 3
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 ^ 3 + 2 ^ 3 + 3 ^ 3 + 4 ^ 3 \\\\
&= 1 + 8 + 27 + 64 \\\\
&= 100
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 ^ 3 + 2 ^ 3 + 3 ^ 3 + 4 ^ 3 + 5 ^ 3 \\\\
&= 1 + 8 + 27 + 64 + 125
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5) ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 ^ 3 + 2 ^ 3 + 3 ^ 3 + 4 ^ 3 + 5 ^ 3 + 6 ^ 3 \\\\
&= 1 + 8 + 27 + 64 + 125 + 216 \\\\
&= 441
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100$

$1 + 8 + 27 + 64 + 125$

$(1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5)^ 2$

$441$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a scrabble tournament, players score $5$ points for a win, $3$ points for a draw and get $0$ points if they lose. 
Part way through the tournament four of the players taking part, players, $A, B, C$ and $D$, **have played different numbers of games 
and have all lost at least one game**. 

Note that there are other players playing in the tournament whose results are not listed here.

**Complete the table below by filling in **all** the missing entries**.

|       |  Played  |    Won   |   Drawn  |   Lost   |  Points  |
|:-----:|:--------:|:--------:|:--------:|:--------:|:--------:|
| **A** |   $12$   |    $7$   | $\ldots$ |    $2$   | $\ldots$ |
| **B** |   $10$   |    $3$   | $\ldots$ | $\ldots$ |   $27$   |
| **C** | $\ldots$ | $\ldots$ |    $2$   |    $6$   |   $41$   |
| **D** |    $8$   | $\ldots$ | $\ldots$ | $\ldots$ |   $30$   |

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The table below shows four different makes of car each with four different engine sizes, in litres, and the Insurance Group for each. For example a Hissan $1.5$ car is in Insurance Group $\text{B}$.

{/*
                ,Car type   ,Skoyota    ,Hissan     ,Foxhall    ,Jagley
Engine size     ,           ,           ,           ,
$1.1$           ,           ,A          ,A          ,A          ,-
$1.2$           ,           ,A          ,A          ,B          ,-
$1.5$           ,           ,B          ,B          ,B          ,C
$1.9$           ,           ,-          ,C          ,B          ,D
*/}

|                 | Car type     | Skoyota     | Hissan     | Foxhall    | Jagley     |
|:-----------:    |:--------:    |:-------:    |:------:    |:-------:   |:------:    |
| Engine size     |              |             |            |            |            |
|    $1.1$        |              | $\text{A}$  | $\text{A}$ | $\text{A}$ |    -       |
|    $1.2$        |              | $\text{A}$  | $\text{A}$ | $\text{B}$ |    -       |
|    $1.5$        |              | $\text{B}$  | $\text{B}$ | $\text{B}$ |$\text{C}$  |
|    $1.9$        |              |    -        | $\text{C}$ | $\text{B}$ |$\text{D}$  |

The second table shows the monthly insurance payment paid by drivers of different age bands for each of the insurance groups, so a person aged $37$ would be in the age band $21{-}45$ and so would pay $\pounds 105$ each month for a car in Insurance group $\text{C}$.


{/*
                    ,Age band       ,Under 21       ,21 - 45        ,Over 45
Insurance Group     ,               ,               ,               ,
A                   ,               ,$\pounds 90$   ,$\pounds 80$   ,$\pounds 70$
B                   ,               ,$\pounds 110$  ,$\pounds 95$   ,$\pounds 85$
C                   ,               ,$\pounds 122$  ,$\pounds 105$  ,$\pounds 98$
D                   ,               ,$\pounds 190$  ,$\pounds 160$  ,$\pounds 170$
*/}

|                     | Age band     |    Under $21$       |    $21{-}45$        |    Over $45$        |
|:---------------:    |:--------:    |:-------------:    |:-------------:    |:-------------:    |
| Insurance Group     |              |                   |                   |                   |
|        $\text{A}$   |              |  $\pounds 90$     |  $\pounds 80$     |  $\pounds 70$     |
|        $\text{B}$   |              | $\pounds 110$     |  $\pounds 95$     |  $\pounds 85$     |
|        $\text{C}$   |              | $\pounds 122$     | $\pounds 105$     |  $\pounds 98$     |
|        $\text{D}$   |              | $\pounds 190$     | $\pounds 160$     | $\pounds 170$     |

**Question**

How much would a $50$ year old person **save** each month by using a $1.1$ Hissan car rather than a $1.9$ Hissan car?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much would a $50$ year old person **save** each month by using a $1.1$ Hissan car rather than a $1.9$ Hissan car?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$1.1$ Hissan car belongs to Insurance Group $A$

A $50$ year old pays $\pounds 70$ under Insurance Group $A$

$1.9$ Hissan car belongs to Insurance Group $B$ 

A $50$ year old pays $\pounds 98$ under Insurance Group $B$

Savings per year by using a $1.1$  instead of $1.9$  Engine Hissan car:

$\pounds 98 - \pounds 70 = \pounds 28$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}           &= 98 - 70 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 28
\end{aligned}
$

The person would save $\pounds 28$ each month.
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\pounds 28$

Now refer to: column Skoyota, Insurance Group $B$ to find the Engine size.
<emptyline>
Group $B$ belongs to Engine size $1.5$
<emptyline>
Same size Jagley belongs to Insurance Group $C$
<emptyline>
Now refer again to Table $2$ column Under $20$ and then Row Insurance Group $C$ to find the amount.
<emptyline>
Under $21$, Insurance Group $C$ pays $\pounds 122$ per month
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In magic squares, the numbers in every row, column and the two diagonals add up to the same number. What is the **sum** of the **missing** numbers in the magic square below?

| $4$             | $8$             | $9$             |
|:---------:    |:---------:    |:---------:    |
|                 | $7$             |                 |
|                 |                 |                 |

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

|                  | $1^\text{st}$ class     | $1^\text{st}$class     | $2^\text{nd}$ class     | $2^\text{nd}$ class     |
|--------------    |----------------    |---------------    |----------------    |----------------    |

**Question**

How much does it cost $1$ adult and $2$ children to travel $2^\text{nd}$ Felby?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>
